&\lesssim\varepsilon\|a\|_{2}^{2}+\|c\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}.\end{split}

(9.74)

Combining (
9.68
), (
9.69
), and (
9.73
), we gain

\begin{split}\|b_{x_{3}}\|_{2}^{2}&\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\varepsilon\|a\|_{2}^{2}+\|c\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2},\quad|G(t)|\lesssim\|f(t)\|_{2}^{2}.\\
\end{split}

(9.75)

9.3.3.
Tangential momentum
b_{x_{1}}
estimate

(Symmetric case)

We try test function
\Psi_{1,1}=\phi_{1}(\mathbf{v})\psi_{1}(x)
, where

\begin{split}\phi_{1}(\mathbf{v})&=(\mathbf{v}^{2}_{1}-\beta_{b})\sqrt{\mu},\quad\text{where}\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{v}^{2}_{i}-\beta_{b})\mu d\mathbf{v}=0,\\
\psi_{1}(x)&=\zeta_{1}(x)D_{1}u_{1},\end{split}

(9.76)

where
\zeta_{1}(x)
to be determined and
u_{1}
solves Neumann problem,

-\Delta_{bel}u_{1}=\frac{1}{\zeta_{1}}b_{x_{1}}-\langle\frac{1}{\zeta_{1}}b_{x_{1}}\rangle:=\widetilde{\frac{1}{\zeta_{1}}b_{x_{1}}},\quad\partial_{n}u_{1}|_{\partial U}=0,\quad\text{where}\quad\langle f\rangle:=\fint_{U}f=\frac{1}{|U|}\int_{U}f.

(9.77)

Above
\phi_{1}
yields only
\mathbf{v}_{1}^{2}\sqrt{\mu}
terms in (
9.56
). With multiplying proper normalization constants to each
\phi_{i}
’s, LHS of (

) becomes

\begin{split}&-\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f(v\cdot\nabla)\Psi_{1,1}dydv+\iint_{\partial U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\Psi_{1,1}^{b}(v\cdot n)dAdv\\
&=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\big(b_{x_{1}}\zeta_{1}D^{2}_{1}u_{1}+b_{x_{1}}D_{1}\zeta_{1}D_{1}u_{1}\big)\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\Big(-b_{x_{2}}\Gamma^{2}_{D,11}-b_{x_{3}}\Gamma^{3}_{D,11}+b_{x_{1}}\Gamma^{1}_{D,22}+b_{x_{1}}\Gamma^{1}_{D,33}\Big)\zeta_{1}D_{1}u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&\quad+C\int_{x_{3}=0}\Big(\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\phi_{1}(\mathbf{v})\mathbf{v}_{3}}_{=0\ \text{oddness in}\ \mathbf{v}_{3}}\Big)\psi_{1}(x)\sqrt{g_{11}g_{22}}dA.\end{split}

(9.78)

(Non-symmetric case 1)

We try test function
\Psi_{1,2}=\sum_{i=2,3}\phi_{i}(\mathbf{v})\psi_{i}(x)
, where

\psi_{i}(x)&=\zeta_{1}(x)D_{i}u_{1},\end{split}

(9.79)

for

\begin{split}&-\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f(v\cdot\nabla)\Psi_{1,2}dydv+\iint_{\partial U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\Psi_{1,2}^{b}(v\cdot n)dAdv\\
&=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\big(b_{x_{2}}\zeta_{1}D_{12}u_{1}+b_{x_{2}}D_{1}\zeta_{1}D_{2}u_{1}+b_{x_{1}}\zeta_{1}D_{22}u_{1}+b_{x_{1}}D_{2}\zeta_{1}D_{2}u_{1}\big)\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\big(b_{x_{3}}\zeta_{1}D_{13}u_{1}+b_{x_{3}}D_{1}\zeta_{1}D_{3}u_{1}+b_{x_{1}}\zeta_{1}D_{33}u_{1}+b_{x_{1}}D_{3}\zeta_{1}D_{3}u_{1}\big)\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\Big(2b_{x_{1}}\Gamma^{2}_{D,11}+2b_{x_{2}}\Gamma^{1}_{D,22}+\underbrace{b_{x_{3}}\Gamma^{2}_{D,13}+b_{x_{3}}\Gamma^{1}_{D,23}}_{=0\ \text{by}\ (\ref {geo cond})}+b_{x_{1}}\Gamma^{2}_{D,33}+b_{x_{2}}\Gamma^{1}_{D,33}\Big)\zeta_{1}D_{2}u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\Big(2b_{x_{3}}\Gamma^{1}_{D,33}+2b_{x_{1}}\Gamma^{3}_{D,11}+b_{x_{3}}\Gamma^{1}_{D,22}+b_{x_{1}}\Gamma^{3}_{D,22}+\underbrace{b_{x_{2}}\Gamma^{3}_{D,12}+b_{x_{2}}\Gamma^{1}_{D,32}}_{=0\ \text{by}\ (\ref {geo cond})}\Big)\zeta_{1}D_{3}u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt+C\int_{x_{3}=0}\Big(\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\phi_{2}(\mathbf{v})\mathbf{v}_{3}}_{=0\ \text{oddness in}\ \mathbf{v}_{3}}\Big)\psi_{2}(x)\sqrt{g_{11}g_{22}}dA+C\int_{x_{3}=0}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\phi_{3}(\mathbf{v})\mathbf{v}_{3}\Big)\underbrace{\psi_{3}(x)}_{=0}\sqrt{g_{11}g_{22}}dA,\end{split}

(9.80)

where we used oddness in velocity integration and Neumann boundary condition.

&\hskip 8.50012pt+C\int_{x_{3}=0}\Big(\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\phi_{1}(\mathbf{v})\mathbf{v}_{3}}_{=0\ \text{oddness in}\ \mathbf{v}_{3}}\Big)\psi_{1}(x)\sqrt{g_{11}g_{22}}dA+C\int_{x_{3}=0}\Big(\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\phi_{2}(\mathbf{v})\mathbf{v}_{3}}_{=0\ \text{oddness in}\ \mathbf{v}_{3}}\Big)\psi_{2}(x)\sqrt{g_{11}g_{22}}dA.\end{split}

(9.82)

9.3.4.
Condition for
\zeta_{1}(x)

), (

(-1)\times
(

\Psi_{b,1}:=\Psi_{1,1}+\Psi_{1,2}-\Psi_{1,3},

(9.83)

to (

),

\begin{split}&-\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f(v\cdot\nabla)\Psi_{b,1}dydv+\iint_{\partial U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\Psi_{b,1}^{b}(v\cdot n)dAdv\\
&=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}b_{x_{1}}\zeta_{1}\big(D_{1}^{2}+D_{2}^{2}+D_{3}^{2}\big)u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}b_{x_{1}}\Big(2\Gamma_{D,22}^{1}\zeta_{1}+2\Gamma_{D,33}^{1}\zeta_{1}-D_{1}\zeta_{1}\Big)D_{1}u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}b_{x_{1}}\Big(2\Gamma_{D,11}^{2}\zeta_{1}-D_{2}\zeta_{1}+\Gamma_{D,33}^{2}\zeta_{1}\Big)D_{2}u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}b_{x_{1}}\Big(2\Gamma_{D,11}^{3}\zeta_{1}-D_{3}\zeta_{1}+\Gamma_{D,22}^{3}\zeta_{1}\Big)D_{3}u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx\\

(9.84)

Similar to the normal momentum estimate, if we match index
3\rightarrow r,1\rightarrow\theta,2\rightarrow z
and pick

\displaystyle\zeta_{1}=\zeta_{\theta}=\frac{1}{r},

(9.85)

\zeta_{1}(x)
satisfies:

\begin{cases}D_{1}\zeta_{1}&=\Gamma_{D,22}^{1}\zeta_{1}+\Gamma_{D,33}^{1}\zeta_{1}\\
D_{2}\zeta_{1}&=\Gamma_{D,11}^{2}\zeta_{1}\\
D_{3}\zeta_{1}&=\Gamma_{D,11}^{3}\zeta_{1}\\
\end{cases},\quad\text{and}\quad c\leq|\zeta_{1}|\leq C,

(9.86)

for uniformly positive
c
and

from (

), (

) and (

). Then above system becomes nearly same as (

) and using Lemma

, the LHS of (

\begin{split}&-\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f(v\cdot\nabla)\Psi_{b,1}dydv+\iint_{\partial U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\Psi_{b,1}^{b}(v\cdot n)dAdv\\
&=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}b_{x_{1}}\zeta_{1}\big(D_{1}^{2}+D_{2}^{2}+D_{3}^{2}\big)u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}b_{x_{1}}\zeta_{1}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{k\neq i}\Gamma_{D,kk}^{i}D_{i}u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx,\\
&=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}b_{x_{1}}\zeta_{1}\Delta_{bel}u_{1}\sqrt{|g|}dx\\
&=\int_{U}b_{x_{1}}^{2}dy-\frac{1}{|U|}\int_{U}\zeta_{1}b_{x_{1}}\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{1}}b_{x_{1}}.\\
\end{split}

(9.87)

With
\Psi_{b,1}
, which is defined in (

\begin{split}\text{RHS of (\ref {glo IBP re})}&\lesssim\Big|\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi_{b,1}\partial_{t}fdy\Big|+\varepsilon\|u_{1}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2},\quad\varepsilon\ll 1.\\
&\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\underbrace{\Big|\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f\partial_{t}\Psi_{b,1}dy\Big|}_{(**)}+\varepsilon\|b_{x_{1}}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2},\\
\end{split}

(9.88)

where
|G(t)|\lesssim\|f(t)\|_{2}^{2}
. Here, we used
\|u_{1}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|b_{x_{1}}\|_{2}^{2}
and
\|\cdot\|_{2}\leq\|\cdot\|_{\nu}
.

To estimate time part
(**)
in (
9.88
), we apply another test function,

\varphi_{1}=\mathbf{v}_{1}\sqrt{\mu}\Delta^{-1}_{N}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}.

(9.89)

When this test function is combined with
\partial_{t}f
, we get

\begin{split}&\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}f\mathbf{v}_{1}\sqrt{\mu}\Delta^{-1}_{N}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}=\int_{U}\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\\
&=\int_{U}\nabla\cdot\nabla\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}+\langle\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}\rangle\underbrace{\int_{U}\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}}_{=0}\\
&=\|\nabla\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\|^{2}_{2}.\end{split}

On the other hand, considering other terms except
\partial_{t}f
,

\begin{split}\|\nabla\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\|^{2}_{2}&\lesssim\Big|\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{v}_{1}\sqrt{\mu}\Delta^{-1}_{N}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\Big(-v\cdot\nabla f+g-Lf\Big)\Big|\\
&\lesssim\Big|\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{P}f+(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f)\ \nabla\cdot\big(v\mathbf{v}_{1}\sqrt{\mu}\Delta^{-1}_{N}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\big)\Big|\\
&\quad+\ \Big|\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{v}_{1}\sqrt{\mu}\Delta^{-1}_{N}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\big(g-Lf\big)\Big|\\
&\lesssim\varepsilon\|\nabla\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\|^{2}_{2}+\Big(\|a\|_{2}^{2}+\|c\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}\Big),\\
\end{split}

(9.90)

where sufficiently small
\varepsilon\ll 1
comes from Young’s inequality and we used Poincare inequality. Therefore we get

\|\nabla\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\|^{2}_{2}\lesssim\|a\|_{2}^{2}+\|c\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}.

(9.91)

Using (
9.91
), we can estimate
(**)
in (
9.88
) by

\begin{split}\Big|\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f\partial_{t}\Psi_{b,1}dy\Big|&\lesssim\Big|\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f\partial_{t}\Psi_{b,1}dy\Big|+\Big|\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{I-P})f\partial_{t}\Psi_{b,1}dy\Big|\\
&\lesssim\|c\|_{2}\|\nabla\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\|_{2}+\big(\varepsilon\|\nabla\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\|^{2}_{2}+\|(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}\big)\\
&\lesssim\varepsilon\|a\|_{2}^{2}+\|c\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2},\end{split}

(9.92)

where we used

\begin{split}&\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f\partial_{t}\Psi_{b,1}\\
&=\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f\partial_{t}\Psi_{1,1}+\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f\partial_{t}\Psi_{1,2}-\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f\partial_{t}\Psi_{1,3}\\
&\leq\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(a+b\cdot v+c\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}(\mathbf{v}_{1}^{2}-\beta_{b})\sqrt{\mu}\zeta_{1}D_{1}\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\sum_{i=2}^{3}\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(a+b_{x}\cdot\mathbf{v}+c\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}(\mathbf{v}_{i}\mathbf{v}_{1})\sqrt{\mu}\zeta_{1}D_{1}\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}}_{=0\ \text{odd in velocity}}\\
&\quad-\sum_{i=2}^{3}\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(a+b\cdot v+c\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}(\mathbf{v}_{i}^{2}-\beta_{b})\sqrt{\mu}\zeta_{1}D_{1}\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\\
&\lesssim\|c\|_{2}\|\nabla\Delta_{N}^{-1}\widetilde{\partial_{t}b_{x_{1}}}\|_{2}\\
&\lesssim\varepsilon\|a\|_{2}^{2}+\|c\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}.\end{split}

(9.93)

Combining (
9.86
), (
9.87
), (
9.88
), and (
9.92
), we gain

\begin{split}\int_{U}b_{x_{1}}^{2}dy&\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\varepsilon\|a\|_{2}^{2}+\|c\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}+\int_{U}\zeta_{1}b_{x_{1}}\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{1}}b_{x_{1}},\\
\end{split}

(9.94)

where
|G(t)|\lesssim\|f(t)\|_{2}^{2}
.

9.3.5.
Tangential momentum
b_{x_{2}}
estimate.

This is nearly similar as what we computed for
b_{x_{1}}
and
u_{1}
case. We define

-\Delta_{bel}u_{2}=\frac{1}{\zeta_{2}}b_{x_{2}}-\langle\frac{1}{\zeta_{2}}b_{x_{2}}\rangle:=\widetilde{\frac{1}{\zeta_{2}}b_{x_{2}}},\quad\partial_{n}u_{2}|_{\partial U}=0.

(9.95)

(Symmetric case)

\Psi_{2,1}=\phi_{2}(\mathbf{v})\psi_{2}(x)
, where

\begin{split}\phi_{2}(\mathbf{v})&=(\mathbf{v}^{2}_{2}-\beta_{b})\sqrt{\mu},\quad\text{where}\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{v}^{2}_{i}-\beta_{b})\mu d\mathbf{v}=0,\\
\psi_{2}(x)&=\zeta_{2}(x)D_{2}u_{2}.\end{split}

(9.96)

(Non-symmetric case 1)

We try test function
\Psi_{2,2}=\sum_{i=1,3}\phi_{i}(\mathbf{v})\psi_{i}(x)
, where

\begin{split}\phi_{i}(\mathbf{v})&=\mathbf{v}_{i}\mathbf{v}_{2}\sqrt{\mu},\\
\psi_{i}(x)&=\zeta_{2}(x)D_{i}u_{2},\end{split}

(9.97)

for
i=1,3
.

(Non-symmetric case 2)

We try test function
\Psi_{2,3}=\sum_{i=1,3}\phi_{i}(\mathbf{v})\psi_{i}(x)
, where

\begin{split}\phi_{i}(\mathbf{v})&=(\mathbf{v}^{2}_{i}-\beta_{b})\sqrt{\mu},\quad\text{where}\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{v}^{2}_{i}-\beta_{b})\mu d\mathbf{v}=0,\\
\psi_{i}(x)&=\zeta_{2}(x)D_{2}u_{2},\end{split}

(9.98)

for
i=1,3
.
We just change role of index
1
and
2
to gain. If we match index
3\rightarrow r,1\rightarrow\theta,2\rightarrow z
and pick

\displaystyle\zeta_{2}=\zeta_{z}=1,

(9.99)

\zeta_{2}(x)
satisfies:

\begin{cases}D_{1}\zeta_{2}&=\Gamma_{D,22}^{1}\zeta_{2}\\
D_{2}\zeta_{2}&=\Gamma_{D,11}^{2}\zeta_{2}+\Gamma_{D,33}^{2}\zeta_{2}\\
D_{3}\zeta_{2}&=\Gamma_{D,22}^{3}\zeta_{2}\\
\end{cases}\quad\text{and}\quad c\leq|\zeta_{2}|\leq C,

(9.100)

with uniform positive constants
c
and
C
due to (
9.5
) and (
9.18
). Considering above three test function cases, by defining

\Psi_{b,2}=\Psi_{2,1}+\Psi_{2,2}-\Psi_{2,3},

(9.101)

we get

\begin{split}&-\iint_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}f(v\cdot\nabla)\Psi_{b,2}dydv+\iint_{\partial U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{b}\Psi_{b,2}^{b}(v\cdot n)dAdv\\
&=\int_{U}b_{x_{2}}^{2}dy-\frac{1}{|U|}\int_{U}\zeta_{2}b_{x_{2}}\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{2}}b_{x_{2}}.\\
\end{split}

(9.102)

With
\Psi_{b,2}
, similar as (
9.94
), we have

\begin{split}\int_{U}b_{x_{2}}^{2}dy&\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\varepsilon\|a\|_{2}^{2}+\|c\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}+\int_{U}\zeta_{2}b_{x_{2}}\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{2}}b_{x_{2}},\\
\end{split}

(9.103)

where
|G(t)|\lesssim\|f(t)\|_{2}^{2}
.

Proposition 9.6
.

Let
U
be an annular cylinder domain (
9.3
) which is periodic in vertical
z
-direction. We assume that
f
solves the linearized Boltzmann equation (
9.1
) with specular boundary condition (
9.2
).
then we have coercivity estimate

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{P}f\|_{\nu}^{2}&\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\|(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{\nu}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2},\quad|G(t)|\lesssim\|f(t)\|_{2}^{2},\\
\end{split}

(9.104)

under the assumption for angular momentum and momentum with respect to
z
-axis:

\displaystyle\int_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\{(y-y_{0})\times z\}\cdot vf(t,y,v)\sqrt{\mu}dydv=0\text{ and }\int_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}f(t,y,v)\sqrt{\mu}dydv=0,

(9.105)

for all
t\geq 0
.

Proof.

From (
9.75
), (
9.94
), and (
9.103
), we have

\begin{split}\|b\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}&\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\varepsilon\|a\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\quad+\int_{U}\zeta_{1}b_{x_{1}}\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{1}}b_{x_{1}}+\int_{U}\zeta_{2}b_{x_{2}}\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{2}}b_{x_{2}},\quad|G(t)|\lesssim\|f(t)\|_{2}^{2}.\\
\end{split}

(9.106)

WLOG, we set
y_{0}=(0,0,0)
and
z=(0,0,1)
in (
9.105
). Then, we can rewrite (
9.105
) by using the global triply orthogonal coordinate system (
9.4
):

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\{(y-y_{0})\times z\}\cdot vf(t,y,v)\sqrt{\mu}dydv\\
&\quad=\int_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(r\sin\theta,-r\cos\theta,0)\cdot vf(t,y,v)\sqrt{\mu}dydv=\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{1}}b_{x_{1}}=0,\\
&\int_{U\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}f(t,y,v)\sqrt{\mu}dydv\\
&\quad=\int_{U}(0,0,1)\cdot vf(t,y,v)\sqrt{\mu}dydv=\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{2}}b_{x_{2}}=0.\end{split}

(9.107)

Therefore, the last two terms in the RHS of (
9.106
) become 0, and then
we obtain

\displaystyle\|b\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\varepsilon\|a\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}.

(9.108)

Combining with Proposition
9.5
and (
9.108
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|a\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}&\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\varepsilon\|a\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2},\\
\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}&\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\|a\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}.\end{split}

(9.109)

From the estimate for
a
in (
9.109
), if
\varepsilon<1/2
, we derive the following as:

\displaystyle\|a\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}.

(9.110)

Using the above estimate and the estimate for
c
in (
9.109
), we can further estimate for
c

\displaystyle\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\varepsilon\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2},

which implies

\displaystyle\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}.

(9.111)

Furthermore, from (
9.108
), (
9.110
), and (
9.111
), we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|a\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2},\\
\|b\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2}.\end{split}

(9.112)

Using the fact that there exists
C_{1}>0
such that
\|\mathbf{P}f\|_{\nu}^{2}\leq C_{1}\|\mathbf{P}f\|_{2}^{2}
, one obtains that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}f\|_{\nu}^{2}\leq C_{1}\|\mathbf{P}f\|_{2}^{2}=C_{1}(\|a\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|b\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|c\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2})\lesssim\frac{d}{dt}G(t)+\|\sqrt{\nu}(\mathbf{I-P})f\|_{2}^{2}+\|g\|_{2}^{2},

where the second inequality comes from (
9.111
) and (
9.112
).
∎

Remark 9.7
.

In the proof of Proposition
9.6
, two terms in (
9.107
)

\displaystyle\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{1}}b_{x_{1}}\quad\text{and}\quad\int_{U}\frac{1}{\zeta_{2}}b_{x_{2}}

are corresponding to angular momentum and momentum with respect to the
z
-axis. Without the assumption (
9.105
), this explicitly shows that an estimate like (
9.108
) cannot be obtained. To ensure that the solution
F
to the Boltzmann equation (
1.1
) converges to the global Maxwellian
\mu
, it is necessary to assume (
9.105
).

Remark 9.8
.

To the best of our knowledge, this result is the first constructive proof of (hypo)coercivity of the linearized Boltzmann equation in the presence of specular reflection boundary, which is not trivial (e.g. a half space). The claim of
[
35
]
could not hold as the angular momentum preserves in any axis-symmetric domains.

Remark 9.9
.

In our proof we heavily rely on the orthogonal coordinate of a single chart. In general, we do not even have a global single chart of coordinate system.
